# AI PPT Templates

**595 free AI-generated presentation templates for any deck. Works with PowerPoint, Google Slides, Keynote, LibreOffice Impress, WPS Office, and more.**

6,603 slides, all generated from scratch — no stock photos, no watermarks, no signup, no account. Every template previews and downloads straight from this page.

## What's inside

| | |
|---|---|
| Templates | **595** |
| Slides | **6,603** (~11 per deck) |
| Format | `.pptx`, 16:9 — with a preview image of every slide |
| Categories | 19 |
| Catalog | [ppttemplate.ai](https://ppttemplate.ai) |
| License | [CC BY 4.0](LICENSE) — free for personal and commercial use |

> **This repository stores no binaries.** It is a catalog: the decks and their preview images are hosted on our own CDN (`cdn.ppttemplate.ai`), so cloning costs a few megabytes instead of gigabytes. Every download link below is a direct `.pptx`.

## Works with

Any tool that opens `.pptx`:

**PowerPoint** (Windows · Mac · Web) · **Google Slides** · **Keynote** · **LibreOffice Impress** · **WPS Office** · **OnlyOffice** · **Zoho Show**

- **PowerPoint / Keynote / LibreOffice** — download the `.pptx` and open it.
- **Google Slides** — `File → Import slides → Upload`, then pick the `.pptx`.

## Bulk download

`index.json` carries every template — title, description, tags, colors, the detail page, and a direct `.pptx` URL — so the whole catalog is scriptable:

```bash
# grab every minimalist business deck
jq -r '.[] | select(.tags | index("Business")) | select(.tags | index("Minimalist")) | .pptx_url' index.json | xargs -n1 -P4 curl -O

# list decks with at least 12 slides
jq -r '.[] | select(.slide_count >= 12) | .title' index.json

# every blue template's detail page
jq -r '.[] | select(.colors | index("blue")) | .url' index.json
```

## How these were made

Each deck is generated end to end: a language model writes the title, structure, and copy; an image model renders every slide as a full-bleed 16:9 design; the slides are packaged into an editable `.pptx`. Nothing is traced or re-hosted from a stock library, and no deck carries a watermark, logo, or attribution mark.

## Contributing

Issues and PRs are welcome — especially:

- **Bad renders.** Open an issue with the template slug; garbled text and broken layouts get regenerated.
- **Missing topics.** Tell us what you searched for and couldn't find.
- **Better metadata.** Tag and description fixes are easy PRs against `index.json`.
